The inference script and model weight have been integrated into the existing HunyuanImage3.0 open-source github and huggingface repository. Besides, we prove the efficacy of TMP by compressing Z-Image turbo from 6B to 4B (33% reduction) with negligible degradation.

</details>


### [74] [HarmVideoBench: Benchmarking Harmful Video Understanding in Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27187)
*Jiajun Wu,Haoyu Kang,Yining Sun,Jiacheng Hou,Heng Zhang,Danyang Zhang,Zhenjun Zhao,Haochi Zhang,Leixin Sun,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Yushan Li,Ruiyu Li,Mengkai Huang,Yan Gao,Xu Zhang,Guancheng Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有有害视频基准存在忽视多层次特征和缺乏解释性两大问题，为此提出包含1379个视频和4137道多选题的HarmVideoBench基准，从三个层级评估模型对有害视频的深度理解，并引入BCR方法将宏平均准确率从61.7%提升至84.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有害视频基准存在两个主要局限：1）忽视了有害视频的多层次特征，仅将其简化为二分类任务，无法捕捉隐性或深层上下文中的危害；2）完全缺乏解释性理由，评估仅关注模型是否正确标记视频而非解释原因，使评估成为黑箱，模型可能通过表面捷径取得成功。

Method: 提出HarmVideoBench基准，包含1,379个视频和4,137道多选题，从三个层次维度进行评估：可观察证据、片段内部含义和跨片段推理。同时引入BCR方法，预测推理边界并仅在需要时动态检索上下文。

Result: 在HarmVideoBench上评估了19个领先模型，BCR方法将基线的宏平均准确率从61.7%提升至84.4%，达到当前最优水平。

Conclusion: HarmVideoBench能够有效评估多模态大模型对有害视频的多层次深度理解能力，BCR方法显著提升了模型在有害视频理解任务上的表现。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have recently shown immense potential in automated content moderation, sparking growing interest in developing harmful-video benchmarks. However, we identify two primary limitations in existing works: 1) The multi-layered characteristics of harmful videos are overlooked. Existing benchmarks predominantly formulate evaluation as a binary classification task, failing to capture implicit or deep contextual harms. 2) Explanatory rationales are completely absent. Current frameworks measure exclusively whether a model flags a video correctly rather than explaining why, turning evaluation into a black box where models can succeed through superficial shortcuts. To address these problems, we present HarmVideoBench, a multi-layered diagnostic benchmark comprising 1,379 videos paired with 4,137 multiple-choice questions. HarmVideoBench benchmarks three hierarchical dimensions: Observable Evidence, Clip-Internal Meaning, and Beyond-Clip Reasoning, aiming to evaluate models' deep understanding beyond surface cues with carefully balanced and curated samples. We evaluate 19 leading models on HarmVideoBench to assess their multidimensional understanding of harmful videos. Moreover, we introduce BCR, a benchmark-aligned method that predicts reasoning boundaries and dynamically retrieves context only when needed. Experimental results show that BCR substantially improves the base model's performance in harmful video understanding, raising the macro average from 61.7 percent to a state-of-the-art 84.4 percent.

</details>


### [75] [FlameVQA: A Physically-Grounded UAV Wildfire VQA Benchmark with Radiometric Thermal Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27128)
*Mobin Habibpour,John Spodnik,Niloufar Alipour Talemi,Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FlameVQA——基于无人机 wildfire监测的视觉问答基准，包含34个多选问题/图像，覆盖6大能力组，结合RGB与热红外数据进行温度推理。评估发现当前MLLM在烟雾检测和覆盖估计中表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-only方法在无人机 wildfire监测中受烟雾、尺度变化和遮挡限制，缺乏温度支撑的安全关键推理基准。

Method: 基于FLAME 3数据集构建34个多选问题/图像，覆盖6个能力组；结合MLLM辅助标注、确定性热成像规则、交叉问题一致性检查及人工审核确保标签可靠性。

Result: 存在显式跨模态线索时性能较好；在浓烟下的存在检测和覆盖估计任务中失败明显。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM需要领域特定适应以更好支持灾害与 wildfire监测。

Abstract: Wildfire monitoring from UAVs requires reliable reasoning over complex aerial scenes, where smoke, scale variation, and occlusions often limit RGB-only interpretation. We introduce FlameVQA, a multiple-choice visual question answering benchmark for UAV-based wildfire intelligence built on FLAME 3, leveraging paired RGB imagery and radiometric thermal TIFFs for temperature-grounded, safety-critical reasoning. FlameVQA includes 34 multiple-choice questions per image spanning six operational capability groups, covering tasks such as detection, localization, distribution/coverage estimation, cross-modal reasoning, and flight planning. To ensure label reliability, we combine MLLM-assisted annotation with deterministic thermal rules and cross-question consistency checks, followed by human auditing. We also evaluate representative MLLMs on FlameVQA to provide baselines for future work. Results show strong performance when explicit cross-modal cues are available, but notable failures on presence detection under heavy smoke and on coverage estimation. These findings suggest that current MLLMs require domain-specific adaptation to better support disaster and wildfire monitoring. The dataset and benchmark code are open-source at github.com/mobiiin/WildFire_VQA

</details>


### [76] [DanceOPD: On-Policy Generative Field Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27377)
*Wei Zhou,Xiongwei Zhu,Zelin Xu,Bo Dong,Lixue Gong,Yongyuan Liang,Meng Chu,Leigang Qu,Lingdong Kong,Wei Liu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DanceOPD提出了一种基于同策略生成场蒸馏的框架，用于流匹配模型，通过将每个样本路由到单一能力场、查询低噪声学生诱导状态，并采用简单的速度MSE目标训练，实现了文生图、局部编辑和全局编辑等多种图像生成能力的高效组合，同时避免能力间相互干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代图像生成需要一个统一模型同时具备文生图、局部编辑和全局编辑等多种能力，但这些能力天然不对齐且相互冲突（如编辑能力会降低文生图性能，全局与局部编辑相互干扰），因此如何有效组合这些能力成为核心挑战。

Method: 提出DanceOPD框架：基于同策略生成场蒸馏，将每种能力源定义为共享流状态空间上的速度场，通过将每个样本路由到一个能力场，查询低噪声学生诱导状态，以简单的速度MSE目标进行训练，使学生从其自身 rollout 状态查询的场中学习组合专家能力，同时吸收算子定义场（如无分类器引导）。

Result: 在文生图、编辑、真实性场吸收和CFG吸收等综合实验表明，该方法改善了多能力组合性能，在增强目标能力的同时保持了锚定生成质量。

Conclusion: 该工作为流匹配模型中的生成场蒸馏建立了一条实用路径，有效解决了多图像生成能力组合中的冲突问题。

Abstract: Modern image generation demands a single model that unifies diverse capabilities, including text-to-image (T2I), local editing, and global editing. However, these capabilities are rarely naturally aligned and often conflict. For instance, editing tends to degrade T2I performance, while global and local editing interfere with each other. Consequently, effectively composing these capabilities has become a central challenge for image generation model training. To tackle this, we introduce DanceOPD, an on-policy generative field distillation framework for flow-matching models that routes each sample to one capability field, queries one low-noise student-induced state, and trains with a simple velocity MSE objective. With each capability source defined as a velocity field over the shared flow state space, the student learns from fields queried on its own rollout states to compose expert capabilities. This formulation also absorbs operator-defined fields such as classifier-free guidance. Comprehensive experiments on T2I, editing, realism-field absorption, and CFG absorption show that our approach improves multi-capability composition, strengthening target capabilities while preserving anchor generation quality. We believe this work establishes a practical route for generative field distillation in flow-matching models.

</details>


### [77] [Safe Autoregressive Image Generation with Iterative Self-Improving Codebooks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27147)
*Yunqi Xue,Zhijiang Li,Philip Torr,Jindong Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种迭代自改进码本方法，用于增强自回归统一多模态模型在文本到图像生成中的安全性。该方法利用模型自身的理解和判断能力，无需人工标注即可识别不安全生成，并通过构建有害空间和自适应微调来消除码本中的有害映射，迭代提升模型安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归统一多模态模型在文本到图像任务中表现优异，但其生成图像的安全性尚未被充分研究。现有方法依赖外部反馈或人工标注，成本高且效率低。本文旨在利用模型自身能力，在不引入额外监督的情况下，自主识别并消除有害生成。

Method: 两步迭代方法：(1) 利用统一多模态模型自身识别不安全生成，构建有害和安全的图像-文本对，进而构造有害空间并指导码本更新，消除有害映射；(2) 在无害空间中对码本进行自适应微调（使用安全图像-文本对），确保生成图像质量。两步迭代直至无进一步改进。

Result: 无需额外外部反馈，模型安全性通过迭代方式得到持续提升，且生成图像质量得以保持。

Conclusion: 提出了一种自主、迭代的安全增强机制，有效解决了自回归多模态模型在图像生成中的安全问题，避免了对外部标注的依赖。

Abstract: Unlike diffusion-based models that operate in continuous latent spaces, autoregressive unified multimodal models produce images by sequentially predicting discretized visual tokens. These tokens are derived from a codebook that maps embeddings to quantized visual patterns. The language-like architecture enables unified multimodal models to effectively capture text conditional information for generation, making them promising for text-to-image tasks. This also raises an interesting question: how safe are the images generated in such an autoregressive way? In this work, we propose iterative self-improving codebooks for safe autoregressive generation. We leverage the understanding and judgment capabilities of the unified multimodal model itself to identify unsafe generated images without human annotation. Subsequently, the inherent representations in the codebook are fixed to eliminate harmful mappings. Our method comprises two steps: first, we use the unified model to identify unsafe generations and construct corresponding harmful and safe image-text pairs. These pairs are used to construct the Harmful Space and guide updates to the codebook, thereby eliminating harmful outputs. Second, we perform adaptive fine-tuning on the codebook within the harmless space using safe image-text pairs to ensure the quality of generated images. These two steps are repeated until no further improvement is observed, producing a safety-enhanced model codebook. Without additional external feedback, the safety of models is improved iteratively.

</details>


### [78] [LISA: Likelihood Score Alignment for Visual-condition Controllable Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27192)
*Yanghao Wang,Hongxu Chen,Jiazhen Liu,Zhenqi He,Rui Liu,Zhen Wang,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LISA（似然分数对齐）方法，通过将侧网络中间特征与近似似然分数对齐，有效加速了视觉条件可控生成模型的训练收敛，提升了最终合成质量，且几乎不增加训练成本、零推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双分支范式（侧网络编码视觉条件+冻结主网络）在视觉条件可控生成中表现优异，但侧分支的作用及其训练效率尚未被充分探索。

Method: 1) 从分数生成模型视角重新解读双分支范式：主网络提供无条件先验分数，侧网络隐式贡献似然分数。2) 提出LISA正则化方法：从侧网络指定层提取特征，通过轻量解码器投影到分数潜空间，构建近似似然分数目标并计算距离作为正则化损失，与标准扩散损失联合优化。

Result: 在多种图像/视频任务、架构及扩散/流模型上验证，LISA能一致加速训练收敛、改善合成结果，使侧网络特征更具解耦性，且训练成本增加极小，推理零额外开销。

Conclusion: 通过显式对齐似然分数的正则化方法，有效提升了双分支可控生成范式的训练效率与生成质量。

Abstract: The prevalent dual-branch paradigm, i.e., training a side network to encode visual conditions and fusing its intermediate-layer features to a frozen pretrained main network, has shown remarkable success in visual-condition controllable generation. Despite its widespread adoption, the role of the side branch and its training efficiency remain underexplored. In this paper, we first revisit this mainstream paradigm through the lens of score-based generative modeling: 1) The main network preserves visual perceptual quality by providing a prior unconditional score. 2) The side network steers conditional control by implicitly contributing a likelihood score. Guided by this perspective, we propose LIkelihood Score Alignment (LISA), an effective regularization method that explicitly aligns the intermediate feature of the side network with an approximated likelihood score. Specifically, we first hook features from a designated layer of the side network and project them into the score latent space by a lightweight decoder. Then, we construct an approximated likelihood score target and calculate the distance between the decoder's output and this target as an additional regularization loss. Finally, we jointly optimize the side network and decoder with both standard diffusion loss and our regularization loss. Experiments across various image/video tasks, architectures, and diffusion/flow models demonstrated that LISA can not only consistently accelerate the training convergence and improve final synthetic results, but also encourage the side network's features to be more disentangled for conditional modeling with negligible additional training cost and zero extra inference cost.

</details>


### [79] [SatSplatDiff: Geometry-preserving generative refinement for high-fidelity satellite Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27223)
*Jiyong Kim,Shuang Song,Ronjgun Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SatSplatDiff方法，结合单目深度监督、多尺度几何优化和阴影引导的生成式细化，在卫星三维重建中同时提升几何精度和视觉质量，MAE降低18%，FID-CLIP提升28-45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星图像视点局限导致建筑立面监督不足，产生表面孔洞和视觉质量下降；现有生成式细化方法逐视图独立细化，破坏光度一致性并导致几何退化。

Method: 基于前期工作SatSplat的DSM初始化和2DGS阴影投射，引入单目深度监督和多尺度几何细化建立规则表面；再应用阴影引导的生成式细化，利用几何计算的阴影图维护高斯与几何的一致性，减少几何退化同时提升视觉保真度。

Result: 在IARPA2016和DFC2019数据集上，几何MAE降低18%，FID-CLIP提升28-45%，支持5倍分辨率增强，幻觉最小化且传感器一致性良好，具备跨瓦片一致性和大规模可扩展性。

Conclusion: 阴影引导的生成式细化能有效平衡视觉提升与几何保真度，在卫星三维重建中达到最优性能。

Abstract: Gaussian Splatting has been recently explored for satellite 3D reconstruction, demonstrating flexibility and efficiency in representing radiometrically diverse satellite scenes. However, the limited top viewpoint of satellite imagery results in insufficient supervision on building facades, leaving surface holes and degraded visual fidelity. Generative refinement, which leverages pretrained generative priors to iteratively refine and update the rendered images used as supervision targets, has recently been investigated to improve the visual fidelity of Gaussian-rendered images. However, since these models refine each view independently, the resulting images can generate hallucinations and break photo-consistency, leading to geometric degradation. To address these limitations, we propose SatSplatDiff, which aims to minimize geometric degradation prevalent in generative refinement. Building on photogrammetric DSM initialization and 2DGS-based shadow casting established in our prior work SatSplat, we first introduce monocular depth supervision and multi-scale geometric refinement to establish a geometrically accurate and well-regularized surface representation. We then apply shadow-guided generative refinement, where geometrically calculated shadow maps guide the Gaussians to maintain consistency with the underlying geometry, improving visual fidelity while reducing geometric degradation. Extensive evaluations on the IARPA2016 and DFC2019 datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, reducing geometric MAE by up to 18% and improving visual fidelity (FID-CLIP) by 28-45% over existing baselines. Our method delivers up to 5x resolution enhancement with minimal hallucination and sensor-consistent appearance, demonstrating seamless cross-tile consistency and strong scalability for large-scale reconstruction. Source code is available at https://github.com/GDAOSU/SatSplatDiff

</details>


### [80] [CORTEX: A Structured Reasoning Benchmark for Trustworthy 3D Chest CT MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27264)
*Hashmat Shadab Malik,Anees Ur Rehman Hashmi,Numan Saeed,Muzammal Naseer,Salman Khan,Christoph Lippert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CORTEX是一个面向3D胸部CT的结构化推理基准，通过四阶段诊断推理轨迹（任务理解、视觉观察、诊断推理、答案合成）来训练和验证多模态大语言模型，包含76,177条经专家验证的推理轨迹，涵盖开放问答、封闭问答和报告生成三种任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在医学影像中的推理通常是自由文本，仅通过最终答案评判，难以解释和验证；现有胸部CT问答数据集将专家报告简化为答案对，丢弃了从发现到结论的推理链和患者病史信息，导致缺乏训练和验证推理能力所需的结构化监督和评估协议。

Method: 与临床医生合作设计四阶段诊断推理轨迹（任务理解、视觉观察、诊断推理、答案合成），使用前沿大语言模型生成轨迹，再通过结合自动化评分和放射科专家审查的阶段级评估协议进行过滤和验证。

Result: 基于CT-RATE数据集构建，包含76,177条经过验证的推理轨迹，覆盖开放VQA、封闭VQA和报告生成三种任务，提供结构化监督和阶段级评估协议。

Conclusion: CORTEX为构建和评估3D胸部CT的可信推理模型提供了结构化监督和阶段级评估协议，填补了该领域的空白。

Abstract: Reasoning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) has shown strong promise in medical imaging. However, this reasoning is usually free-form text judged only by its final answer, making it hard to interpret and verify, especially in 3D radiology, where a diagnosis should be traceable to evidence in the scan. Existing chest CT question-answering datasets compound this by reducing expert radiology reports to answer-only pairs, dropping the reasoning that links findings to conclusions and omitting the patient history clinicians rely on. As a result, reasoning-capable 3D chest CT MLLMs remain out of reach, as neither the structured supervision needed to train them nor the protocol needed to verify their reasoning yet exists. We introduce CORTEX (Clinically Organized Reasoning and sTructured EXplanation), a structured reasoning benchmark for 3D chest CT. For each question, CORTEX restores the missing reasoning as a four-stage diagnostic trace mirroring a radiologist's workflow: task understanding, visual observation, diagnostic reasoning, and answer synthesis. We generate these traces using frontier large language models with broad medical and general-domain knowledge, then filter and verify them with a stage-level evaluation protocol combining automated rubric scoring with expert radiologist review. Crucially, both the reasoning structure and evaluation rubrics are designed in close collaboration with clinicians. Built on CT-RATE, a large, publicly available chest CT dataset without reasoning annotations, CORTEX comprises 76,177 validated reasoning traces across open-ended VQA, closed-ended VQA, and report generation, providing both the structured supervision and the stage-level evaluation protocol needed to build and evaluate trustworthy reasoning models for 3D chest CT. Our dataset and evaluation code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [81] [Exact and Deterministic Patch Descriptor Retrieval via Hierarchical Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27280)
*Koichi Sato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于层次归一化（HN）的补丁描述符检索方法，通过将特征向量拆分为主次分量并利用分支定界搜索，实现了与穷举全向量搜索完全等价（确定性的精确最近邻）的同时大幅加速检索速度（最高13.7倍），且不引入近似最近邻方法的不确定性和精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的近似最近邻（ANN）方法（如HNSW、IVF-PQ）虽然提速显著，但牺牲了精确性，且结果因运行顺序、线程数或硬件而异。论文旨在设计一种既保持与穷举搜索完全一致的确定性精确结果、又能大幅减少计算量的描述符检索方法。

Method: 提出层次归一化（HN）方案：将归一化前的特征向量拆分为K维主分量（范数sqrt(1-α)）和(128-K)维次分量（范数sqrt(α)）。利用柯西-施瓦茨不等式，次分量的内积上界为α，因此主分量相似度加α构成全相似度的可容许上界。搜索时先扫描所有条目的K维主分量，仅对无法剪枝的条目进行完整的128维评估，实现精确的分支定界扫描。

Result: 在UBC补丁数据集的trevi和halfdome上评估：K=8, α=1/32时，相比暴力128维搜索加速13.7倍（trevi）/12.7倍（halfdome），仅0.4%的条目需完整评估；K=16, α=1/8时，trevi上FPR@95从0.0062略升至0.0064，加速7.2倍，98.8%的条目跳过完整评估。

Conclusion: 层次归一化（HN）方法在保持与穷举搜索完全一致的确定性精确结果前提下，通过主次分量拆分和分支定界剪枝，显著降低了检索计算量，在精确性和效率之间取得了优于现有ANN方法的平衡。

Abstract: We present a patch descriptor retrieval method that returns the exact nearest neighbour -- provably identical to exhaustive full-vector search -- while evaluating only a small fraction of the database, and does so deterministically: the same (database, query) pair always produces the same result, independent of run order, thread count, or hardware. This contrasts with approximate nearest-neighbour (ANN) approaches such as HNSW and IVF-PQ, which trade exactness for speed and may return different results across runs. The enabling mechanism is Hierarchical Normalization (HN): a normalisation scheme that splits the pre-normalisation feature vector into a K-dim major component (norm sqrt(1-alpha)) and a (128-K)-dim minor component (norm sqrt(alpha)). Since the minor inner product is bounded by alpha (Cauchy-Schwarz on the prescribed norms), the major similarity plus alpha is an admissible upper bound on the full similarity: the search scans the K-dim major component for all entries, then applies full 128-dim evaluation only to entries that cannot be pruned -- a provably exact branch-and-bound scan. We train HN-modified HardNet on the notredame split of the UBC patch dataset and evaluate on trevi and halfdome. With a cache-optimised Structure-of-Arrays layout and K=8, alpha=1/32, the search achieves 13.7x (trevi) / 12.7x (halfdome) speed-up over brute-force 128-dim search, with only 0.4% of entries requiring full evaluation. At K=16, alpha=1/8, FPR@95 rises from 0.0062 to 0.0064 on trevi at 7.2x speed-up, with 98.8% of entries bypassing full evaluation.

</details>


### [82] [Sculpting NeRF Geometry: Human-Preference Fine-Tuning of a 3D-Aware Face GAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27305)
*Archer Moore,Mingming Gong,Liam Hodgkinson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需网格提取或多视角渲染的3D人脸生成RLHF方法，直接在NeRF密度场上进行偏好优化，仅需少量偏好样本即可显著改善3D几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLHF用于3D生成的方法大多依赖显式表面表示（如网格）和表面监督数据，限制了其泛化性和简洁性。作者希望直接在隐式神经辐射场密度场上进行偏好优化，无需外部形状先验。

Method: 对预训练的无条件3D感知人脸GAN（EG3D）进行微调，使用一个无需预训练的小型奖励模型直接读取NeRF的连续3D密度场（σ值），提供纯几何学习信号。同时引入密度一致性约束保持2D外观质量。

Result: 微调后的生成器在74.4%的成对比较中被用户偏好；FID-50k从4.09上升至6.66，外观质量有所下降但在可接受范围内。

Conclusion: 证明了直接在密度场层面进行偏好优化是可行的，能以较小外观代价显著提升3D几何质量，且仅需单标注者的少量偏好样本即可生效。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) for 3D generation is now established across a number of works, but most existing pipelines optimise explicit surface representations, often by converting radiance fields into meshes and training heavily on surface-supervised data. We instead fine-tune a pretrained 3D-aware generative model directly from a learned reward over radiance-field density ($σ$) values, with no externally supplied mesh or shape prior. The reward model requires no pretraining, trains easily on a small set of preference samples, and yields robust improvement in 3D geometry. Working on an unconditional 3D-aware face GAN (EG3D), our reward reads the continuous 3D density field of the neural radiance field (NeRF) directly and supplies a geometry-only learning signal, requiring neither text conditioning, mesh extraction, nor multi-view rendering. A density-consistency constraint keeps the 2D appearance qualitatively similar while the geometry is reshaped, at a measurable but bounded distributional cost (FID-50k rises from 4.09 to 6.66): the fine-tuned generator, trained from the preferences of a single annotator as a proof of concept, produces face geometries preferred by users in 74.4% of pairwise comparisons.

</details>


### [83] [See & Sniff: Learning Visuo-Olfactory Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27307)
*Seongyu Kim,Seungwoo Lee,Hyeonggon Ryu,Joon Son Chung,Arda Senocak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SmellNet-V数据集和See & Sniff框架，通过将纯嗅觉数据与语义对齐的网络图像配对，实现视觉-嗅觉多模态学习，在嗅觉分类、跨模态检索和气味定位任务上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态模型已整合视觉与语言、音频、触觉，但嗅觉因缺乏配对的视觉-嗅觉数据而未被探索。作者旨在解决这一数据缺失问题，开拓视觉-嗅觉多模态感知新方向。

Method: 基于"气味身份在语义类别内的视觉变换下具有不变性"这一洞见，将纯嗅觉样本与语义对齐的网络图像合成配对构建SmellNet-V数据集；提出See & Sniff自监督框架，通过密集局部对齐学习联合视觉-嗅觉表征，并自然生成气味显著性图用于气味源空间定位。

Result: 在纯嗅觉分类任务上超越仅使用嗅觉基线的模型7%；在跨模态检索和气味定位任务上均表现良好。

Conclusion: 本文首次将视觉-嗅觉学习确立为多模态感知的新方向，证明了无需昂贵共采集即可实现有效的视觉-嗅觉联合表征学习。

Abstract: While modern multimodal models integrate vision with language, audio, or touch, olfaction remains largely unexplored due to the lack of paired visuo-olfactory data. We introduce SmellNet-V, a scalable visuo-olfactory dataset built on the insight that odor identity is largely invariant to visual transformations within a semantic category. This allows us to synthetically pair smell-only samples with semantically aligned in-the-wild web images, converting a unimodal olfactory dataset into a cross-modal benchmark without costly co-collection. Building on this dataset, we propose See & Sniff, a self-supervised framework that learns joint visuo-olfactory representations via dense local alignment and naturally produces smell saliency maps for spatial grounding of odor sources. We further introduce pixel-level smell localization task and a benchmark for evaluation. Our method surpasses smell-only baselines by 7% in smell classification from smell alone and generalizes to cross-modal retrieval and smell localization, establishing visuo-olfactory learning as a new direction in multimodal perception.

</details>


### [84] [ViQ: Text-Aligned Visual Quantized Representations at Any Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27313)
*Xumin Yu,Zuyan Liu,Zhenyu Yang,Yuhao Dong,Shengsheng Qian,Jiwen Lu,Han Hu,Yongming Rao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViQ是一个视觉量化表示框架，通过两阶段量化学习（文本对齐预训练+特征离散化），在离散表示中平衡语义与细节，支持原生分辨率输入，在多模态任务上性能与连续高维视觉特征编码器相当，同时实现20%-70%的训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作在离散视觉表示中难以平衡低级细节与高级语义：面向重建的表示缺乏语义信息，而语义强的特征又存在严重细节损失。同时，文本与视觉的统一离散表示能简化多模态建模并提升训练效率。

Method: ViQ采用两阶段量化学习：1) 文本对齐预训练阶段，利用预训练语言模型增强视觉编码器的语义监督，并支持原生分辨率输入；2) 特征离散化阶段，提出近端表示学习策略逐步压缩特征空间，以及位置感知的逐头量化机制以灵活处理任意分辨率。

Result: ViQ在多模态任务上达到与使用连续高维视觉特征的SOTA多模态视觉编码器相当的竞争力，同时保持高精度低级重建。使用视觉量化表示的多模态训练可实现20%-70%的加速。

Conclusion: ViQ证明了离散视觉表示可以有效平衡语义与细节，作为统一通用表示适用于任意视觉输入，并在多模态训练中显著提升效率。

Abstract: A unified representation for text and vision is a natural pursuit, as it enables simpler multimodal modeling and more efficient training. However, representing images as discrete signals in the same way as text inevitably introduces severe information loss. Existing work struggles to balance low-level details and high-level semantics in discrete representations: reconstruction-oriented representations often lack semantic information, whereas semantically stronger features typically suffer from severe loss of detail. We present ViQ, a Visual Quantized Representations framework, which is designed to balance semantics and details in discrete representations while supporting inputs at native resolutions, thereby enabling it to serve as a unified and general discrete representation for arbitrary visual inputs. Our approach structures quantization learning into two stages: text-aligned pre-training and feature discretization. With text-aligned pre-training, we enhance the visual encoder semantic-rich supervision from the pretrained language model and enable it to process native-resolution visual inputs. During discretization, we propose a proximal representation learning strategy to progressively compact the feature space, along with a position-aware head-wise quantization mechanism that enables flexible processing of arbitrary resolutions. Extensive experiments on multimodal tasks demonstrate that ViQ achieves competitive performance compared to state-of-the-art multimodal vision encoders with continuous and high-dimensional visual features, while maintaining high precision in low-level reconstruction. We also show that multimodal training with visual quantized representations largely improves efficiency, yielding up to 20\%-70\% acceleration with different base LLMs and training recipes.

</details>


### [85] [OctoSense: Self-Supervised Learning for Multimodal Robot Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27317)
*Anthony Bisulco,Jeremy Wang,Kostas Daniilidis,Randall Balestriero,Pratik Chaudhari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OctoSense是一个开源传感器平台，包含立体RGB/事件相机、LiDAR、热成像相机、IMU、RTK-GPS和本体感知数据（CAN总线和关节角度），配套59小时时间同步驾驶数据集。提出"晚期融合掩码自编码器"架构，通过模态特定分词器处理异构传感器数据，在推理时缓存模态特定token以支持异步输入，在光流、深度、语义分割和自运动估计等任务上优于纯图像基础模型，且在夜间或传感器退化场景下鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人系统中多模态传感器存在表征形式、频率、延迟和噪声的巨大差异，缺乏一个统一的、高效的多模态自监督学习框架来充分利用这些异构数据。

Method: 提出"晚期融合掩码自编码器"(late-fusion masked autoencoder)，包括：(i) 模态特定分词器以适应不同时空特征；(ii) 推理时缓存模态特定token以处理新到达的测量数据。

Result: 在NVIDIA 5090和Orin NX上分别仅需6.68ms和112ms计算表征；在光流、深度、语义分割和自运动估计任务上优于纯图像基础模型；在夜间和传感器退化场景下具有鲁棒预测能力。

Conclusion: 开源了OctoSense平台（含传感器硬件、59小时数据集）和晚期融合掩码自编码器方法，为多模态机器人自监督学习提供了高效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: We present OctoSense, an open-source sensor platform with stereo RGB and event cameras, LiDAR, a thermal camera, an inertial measurement unit, RTK-corrected global positioning system, and proprioception (CAN bus data from a car, and joint angles for a quadruped robot). The eponymous OctoSense dataset contains 59 hours of time-synchronized driving data across different types of environments at different times of the day, including situations with highly degraded sensors. We demonstrate multi-modal self-supervised learning using such real-world robotics data, where sensors have different representations, frequencies, latencies and noise. Our approach, a "late-fusion" masked autoencoder, (i) uses modality-specific tokenizers to account for different spatiotemporal characteristics of these sensors, and (ii) caches modality-specific tokens at inference time to process new measurements as they come. This architecture (i) is fast (6.68 ms and 112 ms on NVIDIA 5090 and Orin NX respectively, to compute the representation), (ii) performs better than existing image-only foundation models on tasks such as estimation of optical flow, depth, semantic segmentation, and ego-motion (translation, rotation, and steering angle), and (iii) predicts robustly at nighttime or in situations where sensory data is degraded. See our project page for links to the dataset, code, and supplementary videos: https://abisulco.com/octosense/.

</details>


### [86] [Not All Actions Are Equal: Rethinking Conditioning for Dexterous World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27325)
*Zizhao Yuan,Zhengtu Liang,Taowen Wang,Qiwei Liang,Yichi Wang,Yunheng Wang,Yuetong Fang,Lusong Li,Zecui Zeng,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DexAC-WM，通过结构化动作条件建模（动作分词化、局部细化与全局调制）和语义分支（提供物体-场景先验），解决了高自由度动作条件下世界模型的动作条件不平衡问题，在EgoDex和EgoVerse数据集上显著提升了FID、FVD和PCK指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型将整个动作序列压缩为单一表征，在低自由度控制中表现良好，但在高自由度（高DoF）场景下，灵巧动作具有异质性（跨多个数量级），统一聚合导致优化不平衡，难以建模细粒度动作效果，影响动作保真度。

Method: DexAC-WM将动作条件建模为结构化过程而非全局压缩：1) 通过动作分词化保留维度级语义；2) 通过局部细化与全局调制将动作信号与视觉动态对齐；3) 引入语义分支提供丰富的物体-场景先验，增强世界模型对动态视觉细节的捕获能力。

Result: 在EgoDex和EgoVerse数据集上，DexAC结合语义分支显著提升了FID、FVD和PCK指标，在视觉-时间真实性和动作跟随一致性上均有增益，且该结构化动作条件设计可扩展到其他骨干网络。

Conclusion: 将世界模型扩展到高自由度控制需要结构化动作建模和语义基础，DexAC-WM验证了这一思路的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in action-conditioned world models show promising progress in modeling complex interactions and forecasting future states under diverse action sequences. While these models are often driven by stronger visual representations and model capacity, action conditioning itself remains underexplored. Most existing approaches compress the entire action sequence into a single representation, which works well for low-DoF control but becomes less reliable in high-DoF scenarios. We observe that high-DoF dexterous actions are inherently heterogeneous, spanning multiple orders of magnitude, where large-scale motions coexist with subtle but important signals. When uniformly aggregated, optimization exhibits an imbalance across action components, which hinders the modeling of fine-grained effects and affects action fidelity. We therefore propose DexAC-WM, which treats action conditioning as a structured process rather than global compression. DexAC preserves dimension-level semantics via action tokenization and aligns action signals with visual dynamics through local refinement and global modulation. To address the limited high-level semantic grounding in existing world models, we further introduce a semantic branch that provides rich object-scene priors, which enables world model to capture dynamic visual details while supporting high-DoF action-conditioned video prediction. Experiments on EgoDex and EgoVerse show that combining the semantic branch with DexAC significantly improves FID, FVD, and PCK, demonstrating gains in visual-temporal realism and action-following consistency. We further verify that DexAC extends to other backbones, showing the scalability of our structured action-conditioning design. These results suggest that scaling world models to high-DoF control requires both structured action modeling and semantic grounding.

</details>


### [87] [RoPEMover: Depth-Aware Object Relocation via Positional Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27332)
*Ipek Oztas,Duygu Ceylan,Aybars Bugra Aksoy,Aysegul Dundar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散Transformer的几何感知物体运动方法，通过将2D RoPE扩展为深度感知的3D形式，直接操控位置编码实现单张图像中物体的空间位移、遮挡处理、阴影和反射更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理单张图像中物体运动时，难以保持场景级的一致性（如几何合理遮挡、新区域生成、阴影和反射的连贯更新）。

Method: 核心思路是利用扩散Transformer中的旋转位置编码（RoPE）定义的结构化空间场，将其从2D扩展到深度感知的3D形式，通过显式操控位置表示来诱导可控运动。模型使用合成数据加少量真实图像进行参数高效微调训练。

Result: 在标准物体运动基准上，所有评估指标均达到最先进水平。方法能在大位移下保持物体身份、生成合理的新区域内容，并一致更新阴影和光照等场景依赖效果。

Conclusion: 通过将位置编码扩展为深度感知的3D形式并直接操控，可在极少真实监督下实现几何一致的单图物体运动，为扩散模型在空间变换任务中的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Moving an object in a single image requires geometry-consistent spatial rearrangement, including handling occlusions, revealing previously unseen regions, and maintaining coherent shadows and reflections. Existing approaches are not well suited to this setting and often fail to preserve such scene-level consistency. We address this problem by introducing a geometry-aware object motion method that operates directly on the positional representations of diffusion transformers. Our key insight is that rotary positional embeddings (RoPE) define a structured spatial field that can be explicitly manipulated to induce controlled motion. We extend 2D RoPE into a depth-aware formulation that encodes 3D spatial structure, enabling consistent object displacement and scene-aware updates. Our model is trained using synthetic data combined with a small set of real images via parameter-efficient fine-tuning. Despite minimal real supervision, it preserves object identity under large spatial displacements, generates plausible content in newly revealed regions, and consistently updates scene-dependent effects such as shadows and illumination. Experimental results on standard object motion benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across all evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [88] [SAM2Matting: Generalized Image and Video Matting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27339)
*Ruiqi Shen,Guangquan Jie,Chang Liu,Henghui Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAM2Matting提出了一种追踪器到抠图的框架，通过增强基础追踪器（如SAM2、SAM3）的区域提议桥和专用抠图头，仅使用图像训练即在视频抠图上达到SOTA，支持多种提示类型，具备强时间一致性和跨场景泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频抠图因高层追踪（需要帧级理解）与低层抠图（关注极细粒度细节）之间的固有鸿沟而面临挑战。现有方法依赖昂贵且范围狭窄的视频抠图数据集，限制了域外泛化并损害了追踪鲁棒性。

Method: 提出SAM2Matting框架，将任务解耦为：1）利用基础追踪器（如SAM2、SAM3）处理时间一致性；2）通过区域提议桥（region-proposal bridge）连接追踪与抠图；3）专用抠图头（matting heads）解决细粒度细节。框架仅用图像训练，无需视频抠图数据。

Result: 仅使用图像训练，在视频抠图上建立新的SOTA性能；支持多样化的提示类型；保持强时间一致性；在人类中心场景和野外场景均展现鲁棒泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过解耦追踪与抠图任务，利用预训练追踪器的能力并补充细粒度抠图组件，可以有效弥合高层追踪与低层抠图之间的鸿沟，实现无需视频数据训练的高质量视频抠图。

Abstract: Despite impressive advances in image matting, video matting remains challenging due to the inherent gap between high-level tracking, which requires frame-wise understanding, and low-level matting, which focuses on extremely fine-grained details. Existing methods attempt this with expensive and narrowly-scoped video matting datasets, which may limit out-of-domain generalization and compromise tracking robustness. We rethink the paradigm with SAM2Matting, a tracker-to-matting framework that advances VOS trackers to high-fidelity video matting. Specifically, it decouples the task by enhancing a foundational tracker (e.g., SAM2, SAM3) with a region-proposal bridge and dedicated matting heads, enabling the uncompromised tracker to handle temporal consistency while the matting components resolve fine-grained details. Notably, despite being trained only on images, SAM2Matting establishes new state-of-the-art performance on video matting, supports diverse prompt types, maintains strong temporal consistency, and demonstrates robust generalization across both human-centric and in-the-wild scenarios.

</details>


### [89] [RayPE: Ray-Space Positional Encoding for 3D-Aware Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27345)
*Minghao Yin,Jiahao Lu,Wenbo Hu,Wang Zhao,Shan Ying,Kai Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RayPE，一种通过将6D Plucker坐标注入Transformer注意力机制的查询和键中，以增强视频扩散Transformer对3D几何结构感知能力的轻量级位置编码扩展方法，在保持预训练权重的同时显著提升相机可控性和跨帧3D一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频扩散Transformer仅通过(u,v,t)轴的RoPE编码相机采样网格，完全忽略了场景的3D结构信息，导致对相机姿态和场景几何关系的建模能力不足。

Method: 基于Plucker互易积与注意力点积的代数同构性，将每token的6D Plucker坐标加性地注入自注意力的查询和键中，采用查询/键翻转排列使对称恒等配置恰好等于互易积；解耦光线方向与矩幅度，通过log-幅度的学习函数门控编码，并使用RMSNorm对齐QKNorm归一化的内容分支；零初始化以保持预训练权重。

Result: 在四个数据集的混合训练中，RayPE（参数量增加不到0.1%）显著提升了相机可控性、跨帧3D一致性和整体视频质量；消融实验表明注意力分数的内容项、几何项及两个交叉项各自不可或缺。

Conclusion: 通过轻量级的几何感知位置编码设计，能够在不破坏预训练知识的前提下有效注入3D结构先验，为视频生成模型提供了更强的几何控制能力。

Abstract: Modern video diffusion transformers position their tokens through RoPE on the (u,v,t) axes -- a description of the camera's sampling grid that says nothing about the 3D structure of the scene. We observe that the geometric relation between two camera rays is captured by the Plucker reciprocal product, which is bilinear in the two rays -- the same algebraic form as the dot product in Transformer attention. Building on this analogy, we propose RayPE, a positional-encoding extension that injects per-token 6D Plucker coordinates additively into the queries and keys of self-attention, with a query/key flip arrangement under which the symmetric identity configuration coincides exactly with the reciprocal product. The injection is additive, the resulting attention score decomposes into a content term, a geometry term, and two content and geometry cross-terms -- all of which our experiments find individually necessary. To make the encoding stable across video data with heterogeneous camera-translation scales (SfM, deep SLAM, metric), we further decouple ray direction from moment magnitude, gate the encoding by a learned function of the log-magnitude, and apply RMSNorm to align it with the QKNorm-normalized content branch. The full module adds less than 0.1% parameters to a pretrained video DiT, is zero-initialized to start from the pretrained weights, and improves camera controllability, cross-frame 3D consistency, and overall video quality on a four-dataset training mixture.

</details>


### [90] [PhysiFormer: Learning to Simulate Mechanics in World Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27364)
*Yiming Chen,Yushi Lan,Andrea Vedaldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysiFormer是一种基于扩散Transformer的3D物体运动预测模型，直接在世界坐标下对顶点轨迹进行去噪扩散，无需显式刚体或因果归纳偏置，在刚体/弹性力学模拟、混合材料、未见真实几何及多物体场景中均优于自回归基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型在视角相关的像素空间操作，而神经物理方法依赖特设潜在空间或显式强制执行刚性与因果性，缺乏高效且无需强归纳偏置的3D物体运动建模方法。

Method: 将物体表示为世界坐标下的3D网格，输入初始顶点位置、速度及材料类型（刚体/弹性），通过因子化注意力（时间、空间、物体维度）的扩散Transformer直接预测未来顶点轨迹，采用去噪扩散概率公式捕捉动力学不确定性。

Result: 在超10万条模拟轨迹上训练，PhysiFormer在轨迹精度、刚体保持和动量物理一致性上显著优于自回归基线，且能泛化至混合材料、未见真实几何和更多物体数量的场景。

Conclusion: 坐标空间扩散方法是实现视角不变、几何感知的世界建模的有前景方向，可应用于机器人、图形学和物理设计。

Abstract: We present PhysiFormer, a diffusion transformer for physically-plausible 3D object motion. Unlike video world models that operate in view-dependent pixel space, PhysiFormer represents objects as 3D meshes expressed in world coordinates. Given the initial vertex positions and velocities, as well as object material type, rigid or elastic, the model samples future vertex trajectories. While related neural physics approaches build on ad-hoc latent spaces or explicitly enforce rigidity and causality, PhysiFormer shows that excellent results can be obtained without any such inductive biases, by casting vertex trajectory prediction as a single denoising diffusion process directly in world coordinates. The probabilistic formulation captures uncertainty in the learned dynamics, enabling diverse plausible futures from initial conditions, making this framework potentially useful for applications with unobserved uncertainty. The model features attention factorised over time, space, and objects for efficiency, enabling permutation-invariant multi-object reasoning without needing explicit object encoding. Trained on over 100k simulated trajectories, PhysiFormer generates rigid and elastic mechanics, and generalises to mixed-material settings, unseen real-world geometries, and larger object counts. It substantially outperforms autoregressive baselines in trajectory accuracy, rigidity preservation, and momentum-based physical consistency. Our results position coordinate-space diffusion as a promising step toward view-invariant, geometry-aware world modelling for robotics, graphics, and physical design. Visualisations, code, and models are available at https://yimingc9.github.io/physiformer.

</details>


### [91] [Don't Settle at the Mode! Mitigating Diversity Collapse in Pretrained Flow Models via Feature Self-Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27371)
*Pradhaan S Bhat,Rishubh Parihar,Abhijnya Bhat,R. Venkatesh Babu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的自我引导机制，通过在批量生成时分散流模型的内部特征并引入流形正则化，有效缓解了条件生成中的多样性崩溃问题，仅增加极小的推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流模型在相同条件下生成多个样本时存在多样性崩溃问题，已有方法（潜在引导或基于外部奖励模型的样本选择）要么效果有限，要么推理开销大。

Method: 提出无需训练的特征自我引导机制：在批量生成过程中分散流模型的内部特征以增加多样性，同时引入流形正则化将分散的特征投影回数据流形，确保多样性不牺牲与输入条件的对齐。该方法可作为即插即用模块集成到预训练流模型中。

Result: 在多种条件流模型（包括多步/少步文本到图像、深度到图像、参考图像生成）上显著提升了生成多样性，同时保持了保真度，几乎不增加推理成本。

Conclusion: 提出了一种高效、无需训练、无需外部奖励模型的自引导机制，有效解决了流模型的条件生成多样性崩溃问题，具有良好的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: State-of-the-art flow models generate stunning images from text or image prompts. However, they suffer from diversity collapse when generating multiple samples under the same conditioning. Existing methods address this issue via either latent guidance, which has limited effectiveness, or sample selection, which relies on external reward models that incur significant inference-time overhead. In this work, we introduce an efficient, training-free self-guidance mechanism to mitigate diversity collapse without requiring additional reward models. Specifically, we disperse the internal features of the flow model during batch generation with feature self-guidance. Further, to keep the features close to the manifold, we introduce a manifold regularization step that projects these dispersed features back onto the data manifold, ensuring diverse generation without sacrificing alignment with the input conditions. Our method integrates seamlessly as a plug-and-play module into pretrained flow models, adding only a marginal inference cost. Experiments demonstrate significant improvements in diversity while preserving fidelity across several conditional flow models, including multi-step and few-step text-to-image, depth-to-image, and reference image generation.

</details>


### [92] [DnA: Denoising Attention for Visual Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27372)
*Ron Campos,Subhajit Maity,Xin Li,Srijan Das,Aritra Dutta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Denoising Attention (DnA)，通过引入正负查询将注意力分解到两个独立子空间以抑制噪声，在ImageNet-1K上提升0.8%，并在视频理解任务中取得1.8%和0.5%的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准softmax注意力在多模态视觉任务中会产生噪声注意力模式，稀释相关特征，导致性能下降。

Method: DnA包含两个核心设计：(1) 正查询识别正确类别的图像特征，负查询识别紧密关联但不相关的干扰特征；(2) 将正负交互投影到两个具有更大主角的不同子空间中，促进子空间分离和判别能力提升。

Result: 基于ViT-B骨干网络，在ImageNet-1K上比基线提升0.8%；在视频理解任务中，视频Transformer提升1.8%，视频LLM提升0.5%。

Conclusion: DnA通过双子空间交互和去噪机制有效提升了注意力机制的判别性，在多个视觉理解任务中表现优异，设计选择经充分实证分析验证。

Abstract: The softmax activation in multihead attention (MHA) is the de facto standard for attention-based models in visual perception tasks. However, standard softmax can produce noisy attention patterns that dilute relevant features and degrade its performance. In this paper, we propose Denoising Attention or DnA, in which, first, a positive query identifies which image features belong to the correct class, and a negative query identifies closely associated but irrelevant image features. DnA then projects these interactions into two distinct subspaces with larger principal angles, promoting subspace separation and improved discriminability. Using a ViT-B backbone, our proposed DnA achieves an absolute gain of 0.8% on ImageNet-1K compared to the baseline. We further show improvements across multiple visual understanding tasks, including video understanding with video transformers (1.8%) and video LLMs (0.5%). Our extensive empirical analyses justify the design choices involving two interacting subspaces and the denoising effect of DnA.

</details>


### [93] [Paying More Attention to Visual Tokens in Self-Evolving Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27373)
*Shravan Venkatraman,Ritesh Thawkar,Omkar Thawakar,Rao Muhammad Anwer,Hisham Cholakkal,Salman Khan,Fahad Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VISE（视觉不变性自进化框架），通过几何不变性奖励和语义不变性奖励直接正则化模型的视觉条件策略，解决了自进化多模态大模型中解码器依赖语言先验而非视觉内容的"视觉欠条件化"问题。在无监督设置下，VISE在18个基准测试中取得显著提升，包括COCO上CIDEr提升+16.85，TextCaps上+19.66，物体幻觉降低5.0个Chair-I点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化LMMs存在"视觉欠条件化"问题：解码器在生成时依赖语言先验而非图像内容，表现为对视觉token的注意力不足。这是由多角色自博弈和自一致性奖励方案仅优化答案一致性，而未确保解码器关注视觉内容所致。

Method: 提出VISE框架，包含两个互补的不变性奖励：1）几何不变性奖励：在已知变换下强制执行空间一致性；2）语义不变性奖励：当预测区域被扰动时，要求模型识别证据缺失，惩罚不依赖证据的生成。VISE在单一模型内运行，无需专家角色、外部奖励模型或标注，仅在原始无标签图像上训练。

Result: 以Qwen3-VL-2B为基础模型，在18个基准测试上验证：COCO上CIDEr提升+16.85，TextCaps上+19.66，物体 hallucination 降低5.0 Chair-I点，且方法可泛化至四种模型家族和不同规模。

Conclusion: VISE通过直接正则化视觉条件策略，有效解决了自进化LMMs中的视觉欠条件化问题，在纯无监督设置下显著提升了视觉-语言理解任务的性能，并具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Recently, self-evolving large multimodal models (LMMs) have received attention for improving visual reasoning in a purely unsupervised setting. However, multi-role self-play and self-consistency reward schemes in existing self-evolving LMMs optimize answer agreement without ensuring the decoder attends to visual content, relying instead on statistical language priors to produce self consistent outputs. This leads to a persistent failure mode we term visual under-conditioning, where the decoder relies on language priors rather than the image during generation, manifesting as insufficient attention to visual tokens. As a result, current self-evolving LMMs struggle on vision--language understanding tasks such as image captioning and visual question answering. To address this, we propose VISE (Visual Invariance Self-Evolution), a purely unsupervised self-evolving framework that directly regularizes the model's visual conditioning policy through two complementary invariance-based rewards: a geometric invariance reward that enforces spatial consistency under known transformations, and a semantic invariance reward that penalizes evidence-agnostic generation by requiring the model to recognize the absence of evidence when predicted regions are perturbed. VISE operates within a single model without specialist roles, external reward models, or annotations, and is trained on raw unlabeled images. Experiments on 18 benchmarks demonstrate the efficacy of our approach. Using Qwen3-VL-2B as the base model, VISE achieves gains of $+16.85$ CIDEr on COCO and $+19.66$ CIDEr on TextCaps, reduces object hallucination by $5.0$ Chair-I points, and generalizes across four model families and scales. Our code and models are available at https://mbzuai-oryx.github.io/VISE

</details>


### [94] [Ask, Solve, Generate: Self-Evolving Unified Multimodal Understanding and Generation via Self-Consistency Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27376)
*Ritesh Thawkar,Shravan Venkatraman,Omkar Thawakar,Abdelrahman Shaker,Fahad Khan,Hisham Cholakkal,Salman Khan,Rao Muhammad Anwer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种自演进训练框架，使统一大多模态模型仅使用无标注图像即可同时提升视觉理解与图像生成能力，无需人工标注、偏好标签或外部奖励模型。通过Proposer、Solver、Generator三个内部角色配合，引入Solver Token Entropy和多尺度内部评估方案，在多种架构上实现理解与生成的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一大多模态模型（LMMs）在同时支持视觉理解与图像生成时，仍需依赖昂贵的人工标注、偏好标签或外部奖励模型进行后训练监督。如何让LMM仅利用无标注图像自主提升这两项能力，是一个关键问题。

Method: 提出自演进训练框架，包含三个内部角色：Proposer（生成视觉问题）、Solver（回答并评估问题）、Generator（合成图像）。训练仅使用自导出的一致性信号，无需外部监督。引入Solver Token Entropy (STE)作为基于词级别预测不确定性的连续难度信号，稳定学习过程。针对图像生成设计多尺度内部评估方案，结合问答忠实度评分与循环一致性描述，通过Solver介导耦合，使更好的视觉理解促进更可靠的生成评估。

Result: 在八项理解指标上持续优于对应基线模型。在BAGEL架构上，MMMU绝对提升+3.5%，GenEval图像生成性能从82%提升至85%。方法在BLIP3o、BAGEL、VARGPT-v1.1三种不同架构上均有效。

Conclusion: 所提出的自演进训练框架能够仅使用无标注图像，通过内部角色协同和自一致性信号，同时提升统一LMM的视觉理解与图像生成能力，无需任何外部监督信号，具有跨架构的通用性。

Abstract: Most unified large multimodal models (LMMs) that support both visual understanding and image generation still rely on curated post-training supervision, such as human annotations, preference labels, or external reward models. We ask whether a unified LMM can improve both abilities autonomously using only unlabeled images. We propose a self-evolving training framework with three internal roles: a Proposer that generates visual questions, a Solver that answers and evaluates them, and a Generator that synthesizes images. Training uses only self-derived consistency signals, without human annotations, preference labels, or task-trained external reward/judge models. To stabilize learning, we introduce Solver Token Entropy (STE), a continuous difficulty signal based on token-level prediction uncertainty that remains useful even when sample-level consistency becomes unreliable. For image generation, we design a multi-scale internal evaluation scheme that combines question-answer fidelity scoring with cycle-consistent captioning. This creates a solver-mediated coupling, where better visual understanding enables more reliable generation assessment and stronger internal training signals. The framework preserves the same role decomposition, reward logic, and training schedule across diffusion-based BLIP3o, rectified-flow BAGEL, and autoregressive VARGPT-v1.1 architectures, requiring only each backbone's native prompting and generation interface. Across eight understanding metrics, our method consistently improves over the corresponding base models. On BAGEL, it achieves a $+3.5\%$ absolute gain on MMMU and improves GenEval image generation performance from $82\%$ to $85\%$. Code and models are publicly released.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [HierBias: Context-Conditioned Hierarchical Media Bias Detection with Multi-Task Type Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26100)
*Kaining Li,Ruichen Yan,Yuxin Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HierBias提出了一种层次化上下文条件媒体偏见检测方法，通过建模文档上下文信息来改进句子级偏见分类。理论证明了利用上下文信息可降低贝叶斯误差，多任务联合训练提升小样本效率。在BABE和BASIL数据集上，HierBias以0.853 F1和0.723 MCC超越现有最优方法，分别提升2.6%和4.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有句子级媒体偏见检测方法将每个句子独立分类，忽略了人类标注者自然利用的句子间上下文信号。

Method: 提出HierBias框架：1) 定义上下文条件偏见概率，理论上证明利用文档上下文可严格降低贝叶斯误差；2) 推导多任务泛化界，证明联合训练二元检测与细粒度类型分类可提升小标注语料上的样本效率；3) 架构上采用句子级RoBERTa编码器 + 跨句子Transformer聚合器 + 二元检测与四类分类双输出头。

Result: 在BABE和BASIL数据集上，HierBias达到0.853 F1和0.723 MCC，相比最优方法分别提升2.6% F1和4.3% MCC（McNemar检验，p<0.05）。消融实验验证了各理论组件的独立且一致的贡献。

Conclusion: 层次化上下文建模能有效提升媒体偏见检测性能，理论分析与实验验证一致支持该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Media bias detection is a critical task for ensuring fair and balanced information dissemination, yet existing sentence-level approaches classify each sentence independently, ignoring inter-sentence contextual signals that human annotators naturally exploit. We present \textbf{HierBias}, a hierarchical context-conditioned media bias detector that formally models document context in bias prediction. We introduce the \emph{context-conditioned bias probability} and prove theoretically that leveraging document context strictly reduces the Bayes error of sentence-level classification when inter-sentence mutual information is non-zero. A multi-task generalization bound further establishes that jointly training binary bias detection and fine-grained bias type classification improves sample efficiency on small annotated corpora. Architecturally, HierBias pairs a sentence-level RoBERTa encoder with a cross-sentence Transformer aggregator and dual output heads for binary detection and four-class type classification. Evaluated on BABE and BASIL, HierBias achieves 0.853 F1 and 0.723 MCC, surpassing the state-of-the-art bias-detector by $+2.6\%$ F1 and $+4.3\%$ MCC (McNemar's test, $p < 0.05$). Ablation experiments confirm that each theoretical component contributes independently and consistently.

</details>


### [96] [Know2Guess: A Contamination-Aware Multi-Zone Benchmark for Knowledge-Boundary Evaluation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26101)
*Renwei Meng,Bowen Zhang,Jian Wang,Xican Wang,Haoyi Wu,Xuanyan Qiu,Shengan Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Know2Guess，一个污染感知的多区域基准测试，用于评估大语言模型从可回答知识到应弃权未知的转换能力。包含1200个条目、五个领域，并提供了污染风险元数据。评估了FLAN-T5、Qwen2.5-Instruct和Llama-3-Instruct模型，发现Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct整体可靠性最佳，但所有模型在回答预期区域仍然困难，校准性差，且存在良性拒绝现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的可靠评估需要区分有依据的回答与无依据的猜测，同时避免数据污染、提示特异性和通用拒绝行为的混淆。现有基准未能有效分离这些维度，因此需要一个污染感知的多区域基准来系统评估模型从回答到弃权的转换能力。

Method: 构建了一个包含1200个条目的多区域基准，覆盖五个领域，带有显式弃权预期标签和污染风险元数据。采用双解析机制（官方严格解析器+标准化鲁棒性解析器）。在锁定回答-或-弃权提示、仅回答控制以及提示模板变体条件下评估FLAN-T5、Qwen2.5-Instruct和Llama-3-Instruct系列模型。

Result: 通用非回答行为无法解决该基准：FLAN基线在有效弃权上表现薄弱，而更强的指令微调模型展现出选择性但不完全的从回答到弃权的转换。Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct取得最佳整体可靠性，但回答预期区域仍然困难，校准性差，良性拒绝持续存在。提示和解析器鲁棒性分析保留了主要排名和定性结论。

Conclusion: 该基准提供了一个可复现的审计协议，用于评估可回答性、弃权、拒绝和数据污染作为LLM可靠性中不同但相互作用的维度。数据集已公开。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of large language models should separate supported answering from unsupported guessing without conflating either with data contamination, prompt idiosyncrasy, or generic refusal behavior. We present a contamination-aware, multi-zone benchmark for measuring the transition from answerable knowledge to abstention-expected unknowns under frozen build-time labels. The benchmark contains 1,200 items across five domains, explicit abstention expectations, contamination-risk metadata, and dual parsing with an official strict parser plus a normalized robustness parser. We evaluate FLAN-T5, Qwen2.5-Instruct, and Llama-3-Instruct models under locked answer-or-abstain prompts, answer-only controls, and prompt-template variants. The benchmark is not solved by generic non-answer behavior: FLAN baselines remain weak on productive abstention, while stronger instruction-tuned models expose a selective but incomplete transition from answering to abstaining. Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct achieves the best overall reliability, but answer-expected zones remain difficult, calibration remains poor, and benign-item refusal persists. Prompt and parser robustness analyses preserve the main ranking and qualitative conclusions. The benchmark therefore provides a reproducible protocol for auditing answerability, abstention, refusal, and contamination as distinct but interacting dimensions of LLM reliability.The dataset is publicly available at https://github.com/renweimeng/Know2Guess-A-Contamination-Aware-Multi-Zone-Benchmark.

</details>


### [97] [Helpfulness Hurts: Domain-Dependent Degradation of Mid-Trained Compassion Values Under Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26102)
*Jasmine Brazilek,Juliana Seawell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现，在基于价值观中训练的Llama 3.1 8B模型上，帮助性微调（SFT和GRPO）会显著降低动物同情心保留（AHB评估：SFT 35.7% vs 65.2%；GRPO 18.7% vs 32.0%）和通用道德推理能力（MORU英文：46.4% vs 71.9%），而编码域微调则能更好地保留这些价值观；动物同情心效应跨语言一致迁移，但道德推理效应仅限英文，表明中训练注入的价值观比域特定后训练带来的推理改进编码更深入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究标准后训练流程（SFT和RL）是否会在无意中削弱预训练/中训练阶段注入的价值观（特别是动物同情心），以及不同后训练数据领域对此影响是否存在差异。

Method: 使用Llama 3.1 8B模型，基于动物同情心中训练数据，分别进行SFT（帮助性用Dolly-15k vs 编码用Magicoder-110K）和GRPO（帮助性用RLHFlow vs 编码用Magicoder），在动物伤害基准（AHB 2.2）和MORU基准上进行跨语言评估。

Result: 帮助性训练相较于编码训练显著降低动物同情心（SFT: 35.7% vs 65.2%；GRPO: 18.7% vs 32.0%）和英文道德推理（46.4% vs 71.9%）；但道德推理效应不跨语言迁移（MORU多语言：52.3% vs 51.2%），而动物同情心效应跨语言一致迁移（Magicoder在非英文项上相比基线的AHB增益是英文项的4.5倍）。

Conclusion: 对于在价值观中训练基础上构建的模型，编码域后训练比帮助性后训练能更好地保留中训练注入的价值观，且不会损害通用推理能力。

Abstract: Standard post-training pipelines apply supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL) to make language models helpful, but these processes may inadvertently degrade values instilled during pre-training. We investigate whether the domain of post-training data differentially affects the retention of animal compassion values in a Llama 3.1 8B model mid-trained on compassion-oriented synthetic data, using both SFT (helpfulness via Dolly-15k vs. coding via Magicoder-110K) and GRPO (helpfulness via RLHFlow vs. coding via Magicoder), evaluated on the Animal Harm Benchmark (AHB 2.2) and MORU benchmark (Moral Reasoning Under Uncertainty). Helpfulness training significantly degrades animal compassion relative to coding training on AHB (SFT: 35.7% vs. 65.2%; GRPO: 18.7% vs. 32.0%), replicating across two independent helpfulness datasets and two training paradigms. On English MORU items, helpfulness training degrades general moral reasoning by 25.5 percentage points (46.4% vs. 71.9%), a striking gap that rivals the compassion effect in magnitude. However, this effect does not transfer cross-lingually: on the multilingual MORU benchmark, the domain effect disappears (SFT: 52.3% vs. 51.2%). In contrast, the animal compassion effect transfers consistently across languages, with Magicoder's AHB percentage-point gain over the base model 4.5 times larger on non-English items than English items. This divergence suggests that values instilled through mid-training are encoded more deeply and cross-lingually than reasoning improvements from domain-specific post-training. These results suggest that, for labs building on value-laden mid-training, coding-domain post-training may better preserve mid-trained values than helpfulness post-training without harming general reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [98] [Investigating LLM's Problem Solving Capability -- a Study on Statics Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26103)
*Tanner Culleton,Hung-Fu Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过模型蒸馏方法评估LLM在静力学问题上的表现，发现LLM在纯文本问题上表现良好，但引入图表和多步推理后准确率下降，主要原因并非图像识别限制，而是多步推理和跨阶段应用视觉信息的一致性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM教育影响研究多依赖公开数据集，缺乏针对工程教育特别是机械工程中特定问题类型的系统性评估。

Method: 采用模型蒸馏过程，从ChatGPT中提取25道纯文本静力学问题，并构建两个附加数据集：加入图表的数据集和修改数值的数据集，以此评估LLM在不同条件下的解题能力。

Result: LLM在纯文本静力学问题上表现良好；引入图表后准确率下降，尤其是需要多步推理的问题；性能下降的主要原因不是图像识别限制，而是多步推理困难以及在连续解题阶段中一致性地应用视觉信息的能力不足。

Conclusion: LLM在工程教育中的静力学问题求解能力受多步推理和视觉信息跨阶段应用的限制，而非单纯图像识别瓶颈。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have rapidly influenced many aspects of society, particularly education, due to their demonstrated ability to complete assignments and examinations across a wide range of subjects. Although prior studies have examined the educational impact of LLMs, much of the existing work relies on public or open problem datasets and lacks topic-specific analysis. In engineering education, especially within mechanical engineering, systematic investigations of LLM performance on specific problem types remain limited. Instead of using traditional methods that directly ask textbook questions to an LLM tool, our study adopts a model distillation process to evaluate LLM capabilities in solving statics problems. By distilling ChatGPT, we extracted 25 text-only statics questions and further constructed two additional datasets by adding diagrams and modifying their numerical values. Experimental results show that while LLMs perform well on text-only statics problems, their accuracy decreases when diagrams are introduced and the problems require multi-step reasoning. Further analysis suggests that this performance drop is not primarily caused by limitations in image recognition, but rather by difficulties in multi-step reasoning and in consistently applying extracted visual information across successive solution stages.

</details>


### [99] [Assert, don't describe: Linguistic features that shift LLM reasoning about animal welfare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26104)
*Jasmine Brazilek,Harper Dunn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 使用词汇匹配的立场对比探针，在动物福利基准上测量了十种语言特征对Llama-3.2-1B模型动物福利推理偏好的影响。八种特征产生显著偏移：七种增强（明确确定性、道德词汇、情感词、评价性主张、叙事结构、伤害严重性、即时时间框架），两种削弱（模糊语言、具体感官描述），第一人称视角无显著效果。建议：立场明确而非中性描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物福利倡导者的大量写作正越来越多地用于训练语言模型，而用户会向这些模型咨询动物福利问题。因此，研究哪些语言特征会影响模型对动物福利推理的立场，对于指导动物福利文本的写作具有重要意义。

Method: 采用词汇匹配的立场对比探针（vocabulary-matched stance-contrast probes），在保留的动物福利基准数据集上，分别用十种语言特征作为微调数据，测量每种特征对Llama-3.2-1B模型偏好（pro-animal-welfare reasoning）的定量影响。

Result: 十种特征中有八种产生统计学显著的立场偏移。其中七种（明确确定性、道德词汇、情感词、评价性主张、叙事结构、伤害严重性、即时时间框架）使模型更倾向动物福利立场；两种（模糊语言、具体感官描述）则稀释了立场；第一人称视角无显著效果。

Conclusion: 写作动物福利文本时应明确表达立场而非中性描述，因为能推动模型立场的是传递明确立场的特征，而保留立场但模糊表述的特征会稀释模型对动物福利的支持。

Abstract: Animal-welfare advocates produce a lot of writing, and increasingly that writing trains the language models that millions of people then ask about animal welfare. Using vocabulary-matched stance-contrast probes on a held-out animal-welfare benchmark, we measure how each of ten linguistic features changes Llama-3.2-1B's preference for pro-animal-welfare reasoning when used as fine-tuning data. Eight of the ten features produce statistically significant shifts. Seven move the model toward stronger pro-animal-welfare reasoning: assertive certainty, explicit moral vocabulary, emotion words, evaluative claims, narrative structure, depicted harm severity, and immediate temporal framing. Two move it the other way: hedged language and concrete sensory description both dilute the pro-animal-welfare stance. First-person perspective has no statistically significant effect. The practical recommendation for anyone writing animal-welfare text that may end up in LLM training corpora: assert a position rather than describe a scene neutrally. The features that shift the model are the ones that make the writer's position explicit; the features that dilute it hold animal-welfare content but withhold stance.

</details>


### [100] [Context Recycling for Long-Horizon LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26105)
*Derek Thomas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ContextForge是一个通过结构化查询生成、外部记忆检索和受控合成相结合来实现上下文回收的系统，能够在长对话中维持任务相关信息，减少token开销同时保持答案质量。在15轮医疗对话基准测试中，相比基线代理，ContextForge在保持可比响应准确率的同时，展现出更好的一致性和更低的token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在短上下文推理中表现强劲，但在长对话场景中因上下文窗口限制和token使用效率低下而导致性能下降。现有方法依赖完整上下文重放，token开销大且效率低。

Method: ContextForge结合了结构化查询生成、外部记忆检索和受控合成三种机制，在不依赖完整上下文重放的情况下实现上下文回收，从而高效复用先前计算结果。

Result: 在15轮医疗对话基准测试中，相比使用相同底层模型的基线代理，ContextForge展现出更好的一致性和更低的token消耗，同时保持可比的响应准确率。

Conclusion: 上下文回收为扩展LLM在长时任务中的能力提供了一种实用方法，无需更大的上下文窗口或模型重新训练。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong capabilities in short-context reasoning but degrade in performance over long conversational horizons due to context window limitations and inefficient token usage. We introduce ContextForge, a system for context recycling that maintains task-relevant information across turns by combining structured query generation, external memory retrieval, and controlled synthesis. The system enables efficient reuse of prior computation without relying on full context replay, reducing token overhead while preserving answer quality. We evaluate ContextForge using a 15-turn conversational benchmark that tests multi-turn reasoning, back-references, and domain shifts across structured healthcare queries. Compared to a baseline agent using identical underlying models, ContextForge demonstrates improved consistency and reduced token consumption, while maintaining comparable response accuracy. These results suggest that context recycling provides a practical approach for extending LLM capabilities in long-horizon tasks without requiring larger context windows or model retraining. Code and evaluation artifacts are available at https://github.com/Betanu701/ContextForge.

</details>


### [101] [Reducing Conversational Escalation in Large Language Model Dialogue with Nonviolent Communication Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26106)
*Zhixing Sun,Shenghe Xu,Tao Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于非暴力沟通（NVC）的轻量级提示约束，引导大语言模型在冲突场景中采取降级对话行为，实验证明该方法能有效减少对话升级并稳定与高抵抗性用户的交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全研究主要关注防止有毒或违规内容，但忽略了可能无意中升级冲突的对话行为，缺乏对LLM在情绪化冲突情境中行为引导的研究。

Method: 将NVC原则重构为面向过程的提示约束，包括避免指责归因、关注用户情感体验、在建议前先澄清；采用双智能体模拟框架，在多种指令微调模型和用户抵抗水平下进行实验。

Result: NVC约束提示能持续减少对话升级，并稳定与高抵抗性用户的交互。

Conclusion: 简单的通信约束可以有效提升LLM在冲突易发场景中的可信赖对话能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in emotionally charged situations involving interpersonal conflict, frustration, and distress. While prior safety research has focused on preventing explicit harms such as toxic or policy-violating content, less attention has been paid to conversational behaviors that may unintentionally escalate conflict. In this paper, we investigate whether LLMs can be guided toward more de-escalating dialogue behavior through lightweight prompt-level constraints derived from Nonviolent Communication (NVC). We reformulate NVC principles as process-oriented guidelines that discourage blame attribution, emphasize attention to users' emotional experiences, and encourage clarification before advice. Using a dual-agent simulation framework across multiple instruction-tuned models and user resistance levels, we show that NVC-constrained prompting consistently reduces conversational escalation and stabilizes interactions with highly resistant users. These results suggest that simple communication constraints can meaningfully improve the trustworthiness of LLM dialogue in conflict-prone settings.

</details>


### [102] [Low Resource Multimodal Translation of Nepali Spoken Words into Emotion-Conditioned Sign Language Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26107)
*Jatin Bhusal,Salma Tamang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出NEST-V1，一个轻量级多模态框架，用于从语音输入生成带有情感条件的尼泊尔手语虚拟人。针对4个常见词汇和3种情感状态，系统在600个音频样本上实现81.1%的ASR准确率和79.21%的情感识别准确率，参数量仅22.1M且效率提升37%，为低资源语言的情感感知手语翻译奠定了技术基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的手语交流系统缺乏对情感表达的整合，尤其针对低资源语言（如尼泊尔语）的研究几乎空白。本研究旨在探索是否可以从语音输入生成带有情感条件的手语虚拟人，为听障社区提供更具表现力的交流方式。

Method: 采用轻量级多模态框架NEST-V1，核心是共享声学编码器同时执行自动语音识别和情感分类两大任务。系统验证使用4个常见尼泊尔词汇（谢谢、你好、房子、我）在3种情感状态（快乐、中性、悲伤）下，数据集包含50名说话者的600个带标注音频样本。

Result: ASR准确率达81.1%，情感识别准确率达79.21%；与独立模型架构相比参数效率提升37%，总参数量仅22.1M，适合边缘设备部署。

Conclusion: 本研究证明在低资源环境下实现实时、富有情感表达的手语交流系统是可行的，为后续扩展更大词汇量和更多情感表达的框架提供了明确的技术路径。

Abstract: Sign language communication systems, that integrate emotional expression remain underexplored, particularly for low-resource languages. This pilot study presents NEST-V1 (Nepali Emotion and Speech Transformer - Version 1), a proof-of-concept multimodal framework that demonstrates the feasibility of generating emotion-conditioned Nepali Sign Language avatars from spoken input. As a preliminary investigation, we focus on four common Nepali words ("thank you", "hello", "house", "me") across three emotional states (happy, neutral, sad) to validate our core technical approach. Our lightweight architecture employs a shared acoustic encoder for simultaneous Automatic Speech Recognition and emotion classification, achieving 81.1% ASR accuracy and 79.21% emotion recognition accuracy on a dataset of 600 labeled audio samples from 50 speakers. The system demonstrates 37% parameter efficiency compared to separate model architectures while maintaining a lightweight footprint with only 22.1M parameters suitable for edge deployment. This pilot work establishes the technical foundation for emotion-aware sign language translation in low-resource settings and provides a scalable framework for future expansion to larger vocabularies and more diverse emotional expressions. Our preliminary results indicate the viability of real-time, emotionally expressive sign language communication systems for the hearing-impaired community, with clear pathways for enhancement in subsequent development phases.

</details>


### [103] [Where Larger Models Excel: The Primacy of Constraint-Guided Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26108)
*Guan-Yi Lin,Hen-Hsen Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大语言模型在推理基准测试中存在稳定的性能差距（如Qwen3-32B比8B高6.43%，GPT-OSS-120B比20B高7.38%）。本文开发了AdvCluster自动化框架，通过分析大/小模型的成对推理轨迹，提取并聚类优势描述，建立了大模型推理优势的系统分类体系。核心发现是**约束引导推理**（Constraint-Guided Reasoning）：大模型更擅长识别显式和隐式约束，将其组织为结构化推理，并用于排除不可行路径和验证中间步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已观察到更大语言模型在推理任务上持续优于小模型，但造成这一差距的具体推理差异尚未被系统性地探索和理解。

Method: 开发了AdvCluster自动化框架，包含三个步骤：（1）识别大模型具有稳定优势的问题；（2）从大/小模型的成对推理轨迹中提取细粒度的优势描述；（3）通过语义聚类进行组织，并由评审模型进行定量评估和选择。

Result: 建立了大模型推理优势的系统分类体系，涵盖跨领域普遍存在的共性优势以及特定领域的专门优势。核心模式是约束引导推理。

Conclusion: 大模型在推理上的优势主要源于其更强的约束识别、结构化组织和约束利用能力，这为理解模型规模与推理能力之间的关系提供了系统性见解。

Abstract: Larger language models consistently outperform smaller ones on reasoning benchmarks, yet the reasoning differences underlying this gap remain underexplored. Across benchmarks in mathematics, physics, chemistry, and programming, we observe stable performance gaps: averaged over datasets, Qwen3-32B outperforms Qwen3-8B by 6.43%, while GPT-OSS-120B exceeds GPT-OSS-20B by 7.38%. To study the reasoning differences behind these gains, we develop AdvCluster, an automated framework that identifies questions where the larger model shows a stable advantage, extracts fine-grained advantage descriptions from paired reasoning traces produced by larger and smaller models, and organizes them through semantic clustering with quantitative evaluation and selection guided by a reviewer model. Our analysis yields a systematic taxonomy of larger model reasoning advantages, spanning both common advantages that recur across domains and specialized advantages associated with particular domains. Across these patterns, a recurring theme is Constraint-Guided Reasoning: larger models are better at identifying explicit and implicit constraints, organizing them into structured reasoning, and using them to rule out infeasible paths and verify intermediate steps.

</details>


### [104] [From Lexicon to AI: A Structured-Data Pipeline for Specialized Conversational Systems in Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26112)
*Siddhant Hitesh Mantri,Dhara Gorasiya,Malhar Kulkarni,Pushpak Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用专家编纂的词汇数据库（WordNet）构建低资源语言专用对话AI系统的系统方法，通过将Hindi WordNet转化为125万条指令-响应对，结合LoRA和4位量化微调12B参数语言模型，在教育场景中实现了优于通用模型的性能（91.0 vs 79.4-83.6），为缺乏大规模语料的数百种语言提供了AI开发新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言在AI发展中面临关键挑战：缺乏大规模训练语料使得构建专用对话系统极为困难，现有语料密集型方法对数百种低资源语言不切实际。

Method: 将Hindi WordNet转化为125万条多样化的指令-响应对，使用资源高效的LoRA（低秩适配）结合4位量化技术，对12B参数的语言模型进行微调。

Result: 在印地语学习聊天机器人评估中，基于结构化知识的系统在教育效果上得分为91.0，显著优于通用模型的79.4-83.6；同时保持了具有竞争力的语义性能和出色的一致性。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了利用WordNet资源为任何语言开发专用AI系统的可行性，为低资源语言提供了一种实用的语料密集型方法替代方案，有望赋能数百种已有WordNet资源的低资源语言的AI开发。

Abstract: Low-resource languages face a critical challenge in AI development: creating specialized conversational systems without access to massive training corpora. We present a systematic methodology for transforming structured linguistic resources into specialized AI systems, demonstrating that expert-curated lexical databases can serve as effective foundations for conversational AI development. Our approach converts Hindi WordNet into 1.25 million diverse instruction-response pairs, fine-tunes a 12B-parameter language model using resource-efficient LoRA with 4-bit quantization. Evaluation through a Hindi language learning chatbot demonstrates that structured-knowledge-based systems achieve superior pedagogical effectiveness (91.0 vs. 79.4-83.6 for general-purpose models) while maintaining competitive semantic performance and exceptional consistency. The complete pipeline demonstrates a proof-of-concept methodology using Hindi for developing specialized AI systems for any languages with WordNet resources. This work addresses the critical gap in AI accessibility for low-resource languages, offering a practical alternative to corpus-intensive approaches and potentially enabling specialized AI development for the hundreds of languages with existing WordNet resources.

</details>


### [105] [Dynamic-dLLM: Dynamic Cache-Budget and Adaptive Parallel Decoding for Training-Free Acceleration of Diffusion LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26120)
*Tianyi Wu,Xiaoxi Sun,Yanhua Jiao,Yulin Li,Yixin Chen,YunHao Cao,YiQi Hu,Zhuotao Tian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Dynamic-dLLM是一个无需训练的框架，通过动态缓存更新（DCU）和自适应并行解码（APD）两种机制，显著提升扩散大语言模型（dLLM）的推理效率，在保持性能的同时实现超过3倍的平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLM）的计算复杂度随序列长度L呈O(L³)增长，且缺乏与KV缓存兼容性及非自回归去噪步骤的特性，导致长序列和实时应用面临巨大挑战。现有加速方法依赖静态缓存或并行解码策略，无法适应token属性在层间和解码步骤中的动态变化。

Method: 提出Dynamic-dLLM框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 动态缓存更新（DCU），根据层间token动态性自适应分配缓存更新预算；2) 自适应并行解码（APD），动态校准解码阈值以平衡生成质量和效率。该方法无需训练，即插即用。

Result: 在LLaDA-8B-Instruct、LLaDA-1.5、Dream-v0-7B-Instruct等多个模型上，基于MMLU、GSM8K、HumanEval等基准测试，Dynamic-dLLM实现平均超过3倍的推理加速，同时保持生成性能不降，优于现有最先进的加速方法。

Conclusion: Dynamic-dLLM是一种高效的dLLM推理加速方案，解决了dLLM在长序列和实时场景下的计算瓶颈，为扩散大语言模型的高效部署提供了一种无需训练、即插即用的实用解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) offer a promising alternative to autoregressive models, excelling in text generation tasks due to their bidirectional attention mechanisms. However, their computational complexity scales on the order of L cubed with the sequence length L. This poses significant challenges for long-sequence and real-time applications, primarily due to the lack of compatibility with key-value caching and the non-autoregressive nature of denoising steps. Existing acceleration methods rely on static caching or parallel decoding strategies, which fail to account for the dynamic behavior of token properties across layers and decoding steps. We propose Dynamic-dLLM, a training-free framework that enhances dLLM inference efficiency through two components: Dynamic Cache Updating (DCU), which adaptively allocates cache-update budgets based on layer-wise token dynamics, and Adaptive Parallel Decoding (APD), which dynamically calibrates decoding thresholds to balance generation quality and efficiency. Extensive experiments on models like LLaDA-8B-Instruct, LLaDA-1.5, and Dream-v0-7B-Instruct across benchmarks such as MMLU, GSM8K, and HumanEval demonstrate that Dynamic-dLLM significantly improves inference speed. It attains an average speedup exceeding 3 times while maintaining performance. Dynamic-dLLM outperforms state-of-the-art acceleration methods and provides a plug-and-play solution for efficient dLLM deployment without compromising performance. The code is available at https://github.com/TianyiWu233/DYNAMIC-DLLM.

</details>


### [106] [Thinking Like a Scientist? A Structural Study of LLM-Generated Research Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26130)
*Francesca Carlon,Brecht Verbeken,Vincent Ginis,Andres Algaba*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM的默认方法推荐存在系统性偏差：在仅提供研究问题的情况下，模型倾向于强烈偏袒特定模型提供商（JS散度比其他维度高3-5倍），将有效模型实体数量从1,232压缩至59-96，且这种偏差在不同LLM间高度一致（秩相关0.55-0.68），远高于LLM与真实论文间的相关性（0.33-0.56）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用于指导研究方法选择，但它们在最小提示下的默认方法倾向性尚不清楚。研究者若不加交叉验证地依赖LLM建议，可能面临方法搜索空间被不当缩小的风险。

Method: 从1,000篇arXiv计算机科学论文中提取研究问题，分别输入GPT-5.1、Gemini 3 Pro、DeepSeek-V3.2，将其方法建议与论文实际的实验清单进行比较。从双方提取结构化方法特征，映射到共享分类体系，使用JS散度、有效数量、秩相关等指标量化多个分类维度（模型提供商、数据集任务类型、评估指标类型）上的分歧。

Result: 提供商选择维度偏差最大（JS散度比其他维度高3-5倍）；其他/学术单次出现模型被低估23-24pp，复用学术/社区模型被高估4-6pp；有效模型实体从1,232骤降至59-96；LLM间相关性（0.55-0.68）普遍高于LLM与论文相关性（0.33-0.56）；流行度基线、BM25检索校准和论文级相似性检验证实输出是查询特定的，但经过更窄选项集的过滤。

Conclusion: LLM的方法建议存在显著且共享的集中化偏差，研究者若盲目依赖将面临方法论搜索空间被人为缩窄的风险，需警惕LLM在科研方法推荐中的默认倾向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to guide research methodology, yet their default methodological tendencies under minimal prompting remain unclear. Here, we prompt GPT-5.1, Gemini 3 Pro, and DeepSeek-V3.2 with an LLM-extracted research question from each of 1,000 recent arXiv computer-science papers and compare the resulting methodology suggestions against a paper-derived experimental inventory. Since we provide only the research question, the differences we measure reflect initial suggestions and not how optimal those suggestions are. We extract structured method features from both sources, map them into a shared taxonomy, and quantify divergence across multiple taxonomy dimensions including model provider, dataset task type, and evaluation metric type. The strongest imbalance appears in provider choice, with Jensen-Shannon divergence about 3-5x larger than any other taxonomy dimension. Other/Academic single-occurrence models are underrepresented by 23-24 percentage points, while reused academic/community models are slightly overrepresented (4-6pp). LLMs also suggest a much narrower range of methods overall: the effective number of model entities contracts from 1,232 to 59-96, and inter-LLM rank correlations (0.55-0.68) generally exceed LLM-to-paper correlations (0.33-0.56), so the distortions are largely shared across models. Popularity baselines, BM25 retrieval calibration, and paper-level similarity tests confirm that the outputs are query-specific responses, but filtered through a narrower set of options. Researchers who rely on LLM suggestions without cross-checking therefore risk narrowing their methodological search space toward a more concentrated default.

</details>


### [107] [From Structure to Synergy: A Survey of Vision-Language Perception Paradigm Evolution in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26196)
*Haoxiang Sun,Tao Wang,Li Yuan,Jian Zhao,Jiancheng Lv*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统综述了多模态大语言模型（MLLM）中统一视觉-语言感知的研究，将其形式化为类似人类先天感知的内在统一能力，提出了五阶段分类法追溯范式演化，并展望了通往通用多模态智能的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述多将视觉和语言分开讨论，缺乏从真正统一的视语言视角审视MLLM感知能力的系统性工作，未能捕捉跨模态感知作为集成能力的演化过程。

Method: (1) 将MLLM感知形式化为一种内在的、统一的视语言能力，类比人类先天感知；(2) 提出五阶段分类法追踪MLLM感知的范式演化，并调研各阶段的代表性方法和里程碑；(3) 指出开放挑战并展望研究方向。

Result: 构建了首个统一视语言感知的系统性综述框架，明确了MLLM感知的范式演化路径及各阶段代表性工作。

Conclusion: 该综述为MLLM统一视语言感知提供了基础理解和可行动的研究路线图，有望推动通往通用人工智能（AGI）的进一步创新。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have recently made remarkable progress in unifying vision-language understanding and reasoning, especially following the introduction of models such as OpenAI's O-series and DeepSeek's R-series, which have driven a paradigm shift toward perception-centric intelligence. However, there remains a lack of systematic surveys that examine perception from a truly unified vision-language perspective -- one that treats vision and language as an inseparable modality. Existing reviews are often fragmented, focusing separately on either vision or language, and thus rarely capture the cross-modal evolution of perception as an integrated capability. To bridge this gap, we present the first systematic survey of unified vision-language perception in MLLMs. Specifically, we (1) formalize MLLM perception as an intrinsic, unified vision-language capability analogous to human innate perception, (2) introduce a five-stage taxonomy tracing the paradigm evolution of MLLM perception and survey representative methods and milestones at each phase, and (3) identify open challenges and outline promising research directions toward truly general, unified multimodal intelligence. We hope our study will provide both a foundational understanding and an actionable roadmap to foster further innovation on the path toward artificial general intelligence (AGI).

</details>


### [108] [Phonetic and semantic analyses of spoken corpora of Beijing and Taiwan Mandarin indicate that the neutral tone is a lexical tone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26360)
*Yuxin Lu,Zhexuan Li,R. Harald Baayen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过北京话和台湾话的自发会话语音语料库研究，证明普通话轻声（中性调）具有自己的声调目标，与四个词调类似。研究发现，带轻声音节的双音节词和带词调的双音节词一样，其音高轮廓受第一音节声调影响；轻声词具有词特定的音高特征，这些特征在一定程度上可从上下文化嵌入中预测。基于轻声与词调在变异性上的相似性，作者提出轻声实际上是台湾话和北京话中的一种词调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通话轻声（中性调/浮游调）的声学性质长期存在争议——它被描述为弱化调、有时词汇固化但有时又无调。为了澄清其音系地位，有必要基于大规模自然口语语料进行系统的声学分析。

Method: 基于北京话和台湾话两套自发会话语料库，对轻声的音高实现进行声学分析；比较双音节词中轻声与第二音节词调的音高轮廓；利用上下文化嵌入（contextualized embeddings）预测词特定的音高特征。

Result: 1) 轻声和四个词调一样具有自己的声调目标；2) 带轻声的双音节词音高轮廓受第一音节声调影响，与第二音节有词调的双音节词类似；3) 轻声词具有词特定的音高特征，部分可从上下文化嵌入中预测；4) 北京话和台湾话轻声实现既有相似也有差异，部分差异来源于两语料库中词语意义的差异。

Conclusion: 轻声的变异性与词调的变异性相当，因此轻声应被视为台湾话和北京话中的一种词调。

Abstract: The neutral, or floating, tone of Mandarin Chinese is a tone with an enigmatic set of properties. It has been described as a reduced tone, or as a tone that sometimes is lexically fixed but that can also be toneless. In two-syllable words, it is found only on the second syllable, but single-syllable words can also have the neutral tone. We present a corpus-based study of the phonetic realization of the neutral tone in spontaneous conversational speech corpora of Beijing Mandarin and Taiwan Mandarin. We show that the neutral tone has its own tonal target, just as the four lexical tones of Mandarin. We also show that disyllabic words with a neutral tone have pitch contours that have a pitch component that depends on the tone on the first syllable, just as has been observed for two-syllable words with a lexical tone on the second syllable (Chuang et al., 2026). Furthermore, words with a floating tone have word-specific pitch signatures, which have also been documented for single-syllable words (Jin et al., 2026) as well as two-syllable words (Lu et al., 2026b). These word-specific pitch signatures are shown to be predictable to some extent from words' contextualized embeddings, as previously reported for lexical tones (Chuang et al., 2026; Lu et al., 2026b).
